# Slop draw 1 — the 60 pages, as the local build painted them
Seed 20260906, 20 pages per tier, rendered at 1280 px from `http://127.0.0.1:3141`. Machine-readable twin: `report.json`.
Each page below lists what checks (a), (b) and (c) found first, then the visible text of `main` with the navigation, the footer, the search control and the contents rail hidden, truncated at 700 words. The logical-sequence test — consecutive claims must follow from each other — is the lead's reading, not a measurement, and nothing here attempts it.

---
